<script type="text/javascript">
    // ************ check wishlist collection product to db *****************
    function randomString(length, chars) {
      var result = '';
      for (var i = length; i > 0; --i) result += chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)];
      return result;
    }


    function check_vw_c(cid,pid,indc){
      $.ajax({
        url: checkout_base_url+'/booster/app/wishlist/vw-check.php',
        type: 'POST',
        data:{cid:cid,pid:pid},
      })
      .done(function(data) {
        if(data!=2){
          if(data==0){
            $('.cl_vw_b'+indc).html('<button class="vw_add" type="button" id="wishlist_product" onclick="insert_wishlist_product(this,cid,$(this).parent().next().val(),$(this).parent().next().next().val(),cemail)">Add to Wishlist</button><button type="button" class="vw_remove" id="wishlist_remove_product" onclick="remove_wishlist_product(this,cid,$(this).parent().next().val())" style="display:none;">Remove From Wishlist</button>');
          }else{
            $('.cl_vw_b'+indc).html('<button class="vw_add" type="button" id="wishlist_product" onclick="insert_wishlist_product(this,cid,$(this).parent().next().val(),$(this).parent().next().next().val(),cemail)" style="display:none;">Add to Wishlist</button><button type="button" class="vw_remove" id="wishlist_remove_product" onclick="remove_wishlist_product(this,cid,$(this).parent().next().val())">Remove From Wishlist</button>');
          }
        }

      });

    }



    // ************ check wishlist collection product to db end *************
  </script>
<script async="" type="text/javascript">


    $( document ).ready(function() {
      //console.log('done');
      $( "#email" ).change(function() {
        console.log('change');
        var email_popup = $('#email').val();
        console.log(email_popup);
      });
      $('#SortBy').change(function(){
       
        var curr_url = window.location.href;
        var live = '?q=';
        var sort_check='&sort_by';
        if(curr_url.indexOf(live) != -1){
          var sorting_val=$('#SortBy').val();
          var ff_url =curr_url+'&sort_by='+sorting_val+'#MainContent';
          if(curr_url.indexOf(sort_check) != -1){
            var cc_url =curr_url.split("sort_by");
            console.log(cc_url[1]);
          	var replace_url = curr_url.replace("&sort_by"+cc_url[1], "");
            var ff_url = replace_url+'&sort_by='+sorting_val+'#MainContent';
          }
          //alert(ff_url);
          window.location.reload(ff_url);
        }
      }); 
    });
  </script>

    function setCookie(cname,cvalue,exdays) {
      var d = new Date();
      d.setTime(d.getTime() + (exdays*24*60*60*1000));
      var expires = "expires=" + d.toGMTString();
      document.cookie = cname + "=" + cvalue + ";" + expires + ";domain=.sareeo.com;path=/";
    }
  function filterSystem(minPrice, maxPrice) {
    //$(this).parent('span').addClass('active-filter');
    
    $("div.items").hide().filter(function() {
      var price = parseInt($(this).data("price"), 10);
      return price >= minPrice && price <= maxPrice;
      
    }).show();

  }
    function getCookie(cname) {
      var name = cname + "=";
      var decodedCookie = decodeURIComponent(document.cookie);
      var ca = decodedCookie.split(';');
      for(var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
        var c = ca[i];
        while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') {
          c = c.substring(1);
        }
        if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) {
          return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
        }
      }
      return "";
    }

  function price_incr(product_price){

    var sum_price_perc =  (parseFloat(product_price) + ( parseFloat(product_price) * price_percentage / 100 )).toFixed(2);
    var sum_price = Math.floor(sum_price_perc * convert_rate);
    if(country == 'IN'){
      var final_sum_price = (parseFloat(sum_price)).toFixed(2);
    }else{
      var final_sum_price = (parseFloat(sum_price) + point_price).toFixed(2);
    }
    
    if(final_sum_price == NaN){
    	price_incr(product_price);
    }
    
    return symbol + ' ' + final_sum_price;


  }

  $(document).ready(function() {
    $(".fancybox").fancybox();
  });
    $(".product-form__cart-submitpro"+15).click(function(){
      
	$(this).hide();
    $(".loading_addtocart.product-form__cart-submitpro"+15).css("display","block");

      $.ajax({
        url: 'https://live.sareeo.com/seller/read.php',
        type: 'GET',
        dataType: 'json',
      })
      .done(function(sku_data) {
        console.log(sku_data);
        if(sku_data.indexOf("2372") !== -1){
                            alert('Seller is on vacation, try with other product or come back again soon !');
           
          $(".loading_addtocart.product-form__cart-submitpro"+15).css("display","none");
          $(this).show();
        }else{
           if( country == 'IN' ){
              var product_url_sareeo = window.location.href;
              var product_url_sareeu = product_url_sareeo.replace("sareeo.com","sareeu.com");
              window.location =product_url_sareeu;
            }else{
              $( ".sareeo-add-variants_pro15" ).submit();
            }
     	 }
		});
    });
  
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
  
    $(".product-form__cart-submit"+12).click(function(){
      $(this).hide();
	  $(".loading_addtocart.product-form__cart-submit"+12).css("display","block");

      $.ajax({
        url: 'https://live.sareeo.com/seller/read.php',
        type: 'GET',
        dataType: 'json',
      })
      .done(function(sku_data) {
        console.log(sku_data);
        if(sku_data.indexOf("2290") !== -1){
                            alert('Seller is on vacation, try with other product or come back again soon !');
           $(".loading_addtocart.product-form__cart-submit"+12).css("display","none");
        $(this).show();
        }else{
           if( country == 'IN' ){
              var product_url_sareeo = window.location.href;
              var product_url_sareeu = product_url_sareeo.replace("sareeo.com","sareeu.com");
              window.location =product_url_sareeu;
            }else{
              $( ".sareeo-add-variants12" ).submit();
            }
     	 }
		});
    });
  
</script>

        jQuery(".new_isp_class"+ccc+" .isp_product_price").html('<span style="color:#e8534f;font-family:Work Sans;font-size:14px;">'+price_incr(search_price)+'</span>');
         $('.isp_product_price_wrapper').css('visibility','visible');
          ccc++;
        
      });
    },2000);

// ************ insert wishlist product to db *****************
    function insert_wishlist_product(th,cid,pid,phandle,vw_email){
      $.ajax({
        url: checkout_base_url+'/booster/app/wishlist/sw-insert.php',
        type: 'POST',
        data:{cid:cid,pid:pid,phandle:phandle,email:vw_email},
      })
      .done(function(data) {
        $(th).css("display","none");
        $(th).next().css("display","block");
        vw_count();
      });
    }
// ************ insert wishlist product to db end *************
    

    
// ************ check wishlist product to db *****************
    function check_wishlist_product(cid,pid){
      $.ajax({
        url: checkout_base_url+'/booster/app/wishlist/vw-check.php',
        type: 'POST',
        data:{cid:cid,pid:pid},
      })
      .done(function(data) {
        if(data!=2){
          if(data==0){
            $('.vedaha_wishlist').html('<button class="btn vw_addp" type="button" id="wishlist_product"><i class="fa fa-heart-o" aria-hidden="true"></i> Add To Wishlist</button><button type="button" class="btn vw_removep" id="wishlist_remove_product" style="display:none;"><i class="fa fa-heart" aria-hidden="true"></i> Remove From Wishlist</button>');
          }else{
            $('.vedaha_wishlist').html('<button class="btn vw_addp" type="button" id="wishlist_product"  style="display:none;"><i class="fa fa-heart-o" aria-hidden="true"></i> Add To Wishlist</button><button type="button" class="btn vw_removep" id="wishlist_remove_product"><i class="fa fa-heart" aria-hidden="true"></i> Remove From Wishlist</button>');
          }
        }
      });
    }
// ************ check wishlist product to db end *************
    
// ************ check wishlist product to db *****************
    function remove_wishlist_product(th,cid,pid){
      console.log(th);
      console.log(pid);
      
      $.ajax({
        url: checkout_base_url+'/booster/app/wishlist/vw-remove.php',
        type: 'POST',
        data:{cid:cid,pid:pid},
      })
      .done(function(data) {
        $(th).prev().css("display","block");
        $(th).css("display","none");
        vw_count();
      });
    }

    }

    var vw_Count = getCookie("vedaha_wishlist");

    
    function vw_count(){
      $.ajax({
        url: checkout_base_url+'/booster/app/wishlist/vw_count.php',
        type: 'POST',
        data:{cid:vw_Count},
      })
      .done(function(data) {
        $('.vw_mywishlist').html('<i class="fa fa-heart" aria-hidden="true"></i> Wishlist ('+$.trim(data)+')');
      });
    }
// ************ count wishlist end *************

    function wishlist_product(product,lid,cid) {
     var size_count =0;

        	size_count++;
          	var opt_sel = value.split("Size_");
          	html_option = html_option.concat('<option value="'+opt_sel[1]+'">'+opt_sel[1]+'</option>');
      	}
      });
      if(size_count == 1){
       		var content_html = '<form action="/cart/add" method="post" class="variants clearfix" id="cart-form-'+product.id+'">'+

              '</a>'+

        		'<p class="line-item-property__field">'+
                  '<label>Size</label>'+
                  '<select required  id="size" name="properties[Size]">'+html_option+'</select>'+
                '</p>'+
              	'<input type="hidden" name="id" value="'+product.variants[0].id+'">'+
        		'<button name="add" class="btn" type="button" id="vw_form-'+product.id+'">Add To Cart</button>'+
        		'</form></div>';
	    }
      
      if(product.handle != ''){
       var vw_cpid = product.id;
       var ciid = cid;
        var product_title = jQuery.trim(product.title).substring(0, 60).split(" ").slice(0, -1).join(" ") + "...";
        $('.vw-final').append('<div class="grid__item  small--one-half medium-up--one-fifth">'+

                              		'<div id="ProductCardImageWrapper-collection-template-'+ product.id +'" class="grid-view-item__image-wrapper js">'+
                              			'<img class="lazyautosizes lazyloaded" src="'+product.featured_image+'"/>'+
    								'</div>'+
                              		'<div class="h4 grid-view-item__title" aria-hidden="true">'+product_title+'</div>'+
    							'</a>'+
                              '<dl class="price price--on-sale" data-price="'+product.price+'">'+
                              	'<div class="price__regular">'+
                              		'<dd>'+
                              			'<span class="price-item price-item--regular" data-regular-price="">'+price_incr(product.compare_at_price/100)+'</span>'+
    								'</dd>'+
                              		'<div class="price__sale">'+
                              			'<dd>'+
                              				'<span class="price-item price-item--sale" data-sale-price="">'+price_incr(product.price/100)+'</span>'+
    									'</dd>'+
    								'</div>'+
                              '</dl>'+content_html+
                              '<div class="collection-wishlist">'+

          									'Add to Wishlist'+
    									'</button>'+
          								'<button type="button" class="vw_remove" id="wishlist_remove_product" onclick=remove_wishlist_product(this,"'+cid+'","'+product.id+'")>'+
            								'Remove From Wishlist'+
    									'</button>'+
    								'</div>'+
            						'<input type="hidden" value="'+product.id+'"/>'+
              						'<input type="hidden" value="'+product.handle+'" />'+

                if(get_wishlist_id == ''){
                  setCookie("vedaha_wishlist",wishlist_id,120);
                  get_wishlist_id = getCookie("vedaha_wishlist");
